==========================================================================================

HRS 2018 Final Release 

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquestions@umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

QQ016                         R INCOME FROM SELF EMPLOYMENT - MIN
         Section: Q     Level: Household       Type: Numeric    Width: 6   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecQ.RIncome.Q016_

         (Thinking about your self-employment income in  [Last Calendar Year]):

QQ246_2                       SP AMT RECV FR PENSION - LAST MONTH -2
         Section: Q     Level: Household       Type: Numeric    Width: 4   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecQ.SPPension[2].Q246A

         [Income From Spouse or Partner's Retirement Pensions]
                 
         IF THIS IS THE FIRST PENSION (Q245=1) and THERE ARE AT LEAST TWO PENSIONS
         (Q244>1):
         Tell us about the largest pension that your [husband/wife/partner]
         ([Spouse/Partner First Name] (per X058)) currently receives. How much did
         [he/she] receive last month from the largest pension, (before taxes and other
         deductions)?
                         
         IF THIS IS THE SECOND PENSION (Q245=2) and THERE ARE ONLY TWO PENSIONS (Q244=2):
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from the other pension, (before taxes
         and other deductions)?
                         
         IF THIS IS THE SECOND PENSION (Q245=2) and THERE ARE MORE THAN TWO PENSIONS
         (Q244>2):
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from the next largest pension, (before
         taxes and other deductions)?
                         
         OTHERWISE:
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from that pension, (before taxes and
         other deductions)?
                         
         INSTR: We want the amount before any deductions
                         
         INSTR: Do not probe DK/RF
                         
         Amount:
                 
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:
         [Income From Spouse or Partner's Retirement Pensions]
         
         IF THIS IS THE FIRST PENSION (Q245=1) and THERE ARE AT LEAST TWO PENSIONS
         (Q244>1):
         Tell us about the largest pension that your [husband/wife/partner]
         ([Spouse/Partner First Name] (per X058)) currently receives. How much did
         [he/she] receive last month from the largest pension, before taxes and other
         deductions?
                 
         IF THIS IS THE SECOND PENSION (Q245=2) and THERE ARE ONLY TWO PENSIONS (Q244=2):
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from the other pension, before taxes
         and other deductions?
                 
         IF THIS IS THE SECOND PENSION (Q245=2) and THERE ARE MORE THAN TWO PENSIONS
         (Q244>2):
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from the next largest pension, before
         taxes and other deductions?
                         
         OTHERWISE:
         How much did [he/she] receive last month from that pension, before taxes and
         other deductions?

QQ265                         SP ALL OTH PENS AMT REC - MIN
         Section: Q     Level: Household       Type: Numeric    Width: 3   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecQ.SPOtherPension.Q265_

         (Thinking about  [his/her] income from any other retirement pensions last
         month):
         
         Did it amount to less than $____ , more than $____ , or what?
         
         PROCEDURE: 1Up1Down
         BREAKPOINTS: $50, $150, $400
         ENTRY POINT: $15
         
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:

         (Thinking about the income received  from those other sources in [Last Calendar
         Year]):
         
         Did it amount to a total of less than $____ , more than $____ , or what?
         
         PROCEDURES: 2Up1Down, 1Up2Down
         BREAKPOINTS: $1,000, $3,000, $8,000, $30,000
         RANDOM ENTRY POINT ASSIGNMENT [1 ($3,000)] or [{NOT 1} ($8,000)] AT X508
         
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:
         Thinking about the income received  from those other sources in [Last Calendar
         Year]:
         
         Did it amount to a total of less than $____ , more than $____ , or what?

         User Note: The procedures apply to both the interviewer-administered and
         web-administered text.

         IF SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> NO AND SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> SpecialAnswer AND 
         SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> Empty THEN 
         For SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.i := 1 to W781_numberOfYears do 
         IF SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.yearLoop >= SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.W783_startYear AND 
         SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.yearLoop <= SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.W785_endYear THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.W779_MonthsPerYear[i].W776_Year := 
         SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.yearLoop 

         - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
